```python
"""
Search for a low-peak nonnegative step-height sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

Strategy:
1. Start from the supplied parent when available.
2. Generate diverse parametric/log-modulated starts, including perturbations of
   the parent low-d modes and global random/Sobol-free differential evolution.
3. Use a fast exact local search with O(n) move-update formulas for mass moves.
   It minimizes the exact convolution peak lexicographically: first max peak,
   then sum of the current top peaks.
4. Interleave a KL/exponentiated-gradient projector with line-search acceptance.
   The gradient uses a top-peak softmax over convolution lags and is evaluated
   with FFT.
5. Keep the best sequence by score throughout, with time guarders.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import scipy.optimize as sopt
except Exception:
    sopt = None

_basis_cache = {}


def norm_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def fast_autocorr(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    m = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)[:L]


def score_normalized(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    B = fast_autocorr(a)
    if B.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * float(B.max()))


def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    old_n = f.size
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if old_n == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    if old_n == new_n:
        return f.copy()
    s = float(f.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    fn = f / s
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    fi = np.interp(new_x, old_x, fn)
    fi = np.maximum(fi, 0.0)
    t = float(fi.sum())
    if t <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    return fi / t


def default_sequence(n):
    if n <= 0:
        n = 6700
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    h = 0.85 * np.cos(np.pi * x) + 0.35 * (0.5 - x)
    h = h - h.mean()
    hr = float(h.max() - h.min())
    if hr > 3.5:
        h = h * (3.5 / hr)
    q = np.exp(h)
    q = np.maximum(q, 1e-12)
    return norm_sum1(q)


def _add_feature(feats, arr):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    arr = arr - arr.mean()
    sd = float(arr.std())
    if sd > 1e-12:
        arr = arr / sd
    else:
        arr = np.zeros_like(arr)
    feats.append(arr)


def get_basis(n, mode="mixed"):
    key = (int(n), str(mode))
    if key in _basis_cache:
        return _basis_cache[key]
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    feats = []
    # Useful anti-symmetric features under x -> 1-x.
    _add_feature(feats, np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x))
    _add_feature(feats, np.cos(np.pi * x))
    _add_feature(feats, x - 0.5)
    _add_feature(feats, np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x))
    _add_feature(feats, np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x))
    if mode == "mixed":
        # Symmetric and higher anti-symmetric features.
        _add_feature(feats, np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x))
        _add_feature(feats, np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x))
        _add_feature(feats, (x - 0.5) ** 2)
        _add_feature(feats, np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * x))
    if not feats:
        basis = np.zeros((1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        basis = np.vstack(feats).astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    _basis_cache[key] = basis
    return basis


def param_to_seq(theta, n, basis, Hclip=3.5):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return default_sequence(6700)
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if theta.size != basis.shape[0]:
        theta = np.resize(theta, basis.shape[0])
    theta = np.clip(theta, -10.0, 10.0)
    try:
        h = np.tensordot(theta, basis, axes=(0, 0))
    except Exception:
        return default_sequence(n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return default_sequence(n)
    h = h - h.mean()
    hr = float(h.max() - h.min())
    if hr > Hclip and hr > 1e-12:
        h = h * (Hclip / hr)
    q = np.exp(h)
    q = np.maximum(q, 1e-12)
    return norm_sum1(q)


def score_param(theta, n, basis, Hclip=3.5):
    seq = param_to_seq(theta, n, basis, Hclip)
    return score_normalized(seq)


def de_objective(theta, n, basis, Hclip):
    return score_param(theta, n, basis, Hclip)


def parent_perturb_seq(base, theta, basis, Hpert=2.0):
    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if base.size != basis.shape[1]:
        return norm_sum1(base)
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if theta.size != basis.shape[0]:
        theta = np.resize(theta, basis.shape[0])
    theta = np.clip(theta, -10.0, 10.0)
    try:
        h = np.tensordot(theta, basis, axes=(0, 0))
    except Exception:
        return norm_sum1(base)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return norm_sum1(base)
    h = h - h.mean()
    hr = float(h.max() - h.min())
    if hr > Hpert and hr > 1e-12:
        h = h * (Hpert / hr)
    q = base * np.exp(h)
    q = np.maximum(q, 1e-12)
    return norm_sum1(q)


def candidate_key(item):
    return item[1]


def source_key(item):
    return item[0]


def add_candidate(pool, a, label):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    a = norm_sum1(a)
    sc = score_normalized(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        pool.append((a, float(sc), str(label)))


def eval_move(B, a, tmp, sec_idx, src, dst, delta, n):
    tmp[:] = B
    add = 2.0 * delta
    tmp[dst:dst + n] += add * a
    tmp[src:src + n] -= add * a
    d2 = delta * delta
    tmp[2 * dst] += d2
    tmp[2 * src] += d2
    tmp[src + dst] -= 2.0 * d2
    m = float(np.max(tmp))
    if sec_idx.size:
        sec = float(np.sum(tmp[sec_idx]))
    else:
        sec = 0.0
    return m, sec


def golden_search_move(B, a, tmp, sec_idx, src, dst, n, iterations=8):
    delta_max = 0.8 * a[src]
    if delta_max <= 1e-18:
        return float("inf"), float("inf"), 0.0

    low = 0.0
    high = float(delta_max)
    gr = 0.6180339887498949

    d1 = high - gr * (high - low)
    d2 = low + gr * (high - low)
    f1, s1 = eval_move(B, a, tmp, sec_idx, src, dst, d1, n)
    f2, s2 = eval_move(B, a, tmp, sec_idx, src, dst, d2, n)

    bm = f1
    bs = s1
    bd = d1
    if f2 < bm - 1e-17 or (f2 <= bm + 1e-15 and s2 < bs - 1e-18):
        bm, bs, bd = f2, s2, d2

    for _ in range(int(iterations)):
        if high - low <= 1e-14 * max(1.0, float(delta_max)):
            break
        if f1 < f2 - 1e-17:
            high = d2
            d2 = d1
            f2 = f1
            s2 = s1
            d1 = high - gr * (high - low)
            if d1 < 0.0:
                d1 = 0.0
            if d1 >= high:
                d1 = 0.5 * high
            f1, s1 = eval_move(B, a, tmp, sec_idx, src, dst, d1, n)
        elif f2 < f1 - 1e-17:
            low = d1
            d1 = d2
            f1 = f2
            s1 = s2
            d2 = low + gr * (high - low)
            if d2 > high:
                d2 = high
            if d2 <= low:
                d2 = low + 0.5 * (high - low)
            f2, s2 = eval_move(B, a, tmp, sec_idx, src, dst, d2, n)
        else:
            mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
            half = 0.1 * (high - low)
            d1 = max(low, mid - half)
            d2 = min(high, mid + half)
            f1, s1 = eval_move(B, a, tmp, sec_idx, src, dst, d1, n)
            f2, s2 = eval_move(B, a, tmp, sec_idx, src, dst, d2, n)

        if f1 < bm - 1e-17 or (f1 <= bm + 1e-15 and s1 < bs - 1e-18):
            bm, bs, bd = f1, s1, d1
        if f2 < bm - 1e-17 or (f2 <= bm + 1e-15 and s2 < bs - 1e-18):
            bm, bs, bd = f2, s2, d2

    mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
    fm, sm = eval_move(B, a, tmp, sec_idx, src, dst, mid, n)
    if fm < bm - 1e-17 or (fm <= bm + 1e-15 and sm < bs - 1e-18):
        bm, bs, bd = fm, sm, mid

    return bm, bs, bd


def local_search(a0, time_limit, verbose=True, seed=0, recompute_every=60):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    L = 2 * n - 1
    B = fast_autocorr(a)
    cur_obj = float(B.max())
    best_obj = cur_obj
    best_a = a.copy()
    obj_tol = 1e-15
    near_tol = 1e-13
    sec_tol = 1e-18

    top_count = int(min(80, max(20, n // 40)))
    peak_sources = 8
    src_pairs = 5
    dst_candidates = 25
    quick_pairs = max(60, min(240, n // 30))
    fracs = (0.01, 0.03, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50)
    gold_iterations = 8
    max_no_internal = 800
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    tmp = np.empty(L, dtype=np.float64)
    accepts = 0
    no_internal = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit and no_internal < max_no_internal:
        if accepts > 0 and accepts % recompute_every == 0:
            B = fast_autocorr(a)
            cur_obj = float(B.max())
            if cur_obj < best_obj - obj_tol:
                best_obj = cur_obj
                best_a = a.copy()

        if L <= top_count:
            top_idx = np.argsort(B)[::-1]
        else:
            part = np.argpartition(B, L - top_count)
            top_part = part[L - top_count:]
            top_idx = top_part[np.argsort(B[top_part])[::-1]]

        sec_idx = top_idx[:min(8, len(top_idx))]
        cur_sec = float(np.sum(B[sec_idx])) if sec_idx.size else 0.0

        inf = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        inf_len = min(15, len(top_idx))
        for kk in top_idx[:inf_len]:
            kk = int(kk)
            start = max(0, kk - n + 1)
            end = min(n - 1, kk)
            if start <= end:
                jj = np.arange(start, end + 1)
                inf[jj] += a[kk - jj]

        cand_dst = np.argsort(inf)[:dst_candidates]

        src_vals = []
        for kk in top_idx[:peak_sources]:
            kk = int(kk)
            start = max(0, kk - n + 1)
            end = min(n - 1, kk)
            if start > end:
                continue
            idxs = np.arange(start, end + 1)
            prods = a[idxs] * a[kk - idxs]
            plen = prods.size
            if plen > src_pairs:
                ppart = np.argpartition(prods, plen - src_pairs)[plen - src_pairs:]
            else:
                ppart = np.arange(plen)
            for pi in ppart:
                i = int(idxs[pi])
                p = kk - i
                if 0 <= p < n:
                    if a[i] >= a[p]:
                        src_vals.append((float(a[i]), i))
                        src_vals.append((float(a[p]), p))
                    else:
                        src_vals.append((float(a[p]), p))
                        src_vals.append((float(a[i]), i))
            i_max = int(idxs[np.argmax(a[idxs])])
            src_vals.append((float(a[i_max]), i_max))

        src_vals.sort(key=source_key, reverse=True)
        src_list = []
        seen = set()
        for val, i in src_vals:
            i = int(i)
            if i in seen:
                continue
            if a[i] > 1e-15:
                seen.add(i)
                src_list.append(i)
                if len(src_list) >= 8:
                    break

        if not src_list:
            no_internal += 1
            continue

        pairs = []
        pseen = set()
        for src in src_list:
            src = int(src)
            if a[src] <= 1e-15:
                continue
            for dst in cand_dst[:15]:
                dst = int(dst)
                if dst == src:
                    continue
                tup = (src, dst)
                if tup not in pseen:
                    pseen.add(tup)
                    pairs.append(tup)
            if len(pairs) >= 160:
                break

        if not pairs:
            no_internal += 1
            continue

        quick = []
        for src, dst in pairs[:quick_pairs]:
            delta = 0.03 * a[src]
            if delta <= 1e-18:
                continue
            m, sec = eval_move(B, a, tmp, sec_idx, src, dst, delta, n)
            quick.append((m, sec, src, dst))

        if not quick:
            no_internal += 1
            continue

        quick.sort()
        top_pairs = quick[:25]

        bm = float("inf")
        bs = float("inf")
        bsrc = -1
        bdst = -1
        bd = 0.0

        for src, dst in top_pairs:
            src = int(src)
            dst = int(dst)
            for frac in fracs:
                delta = frac * a[src]
                if delta <= 1e-18:
                    continue
                m, sec = eval_move(B, a, tmp, sec_idx, src, dst, delta, n)
                if m < bm - obj_tol or (m <= bm + near_tol and sec < bs - sec_tol):
                    bm, bs, bsrc, bdst, bd = m, sec, src, dst, delta

        for src, dst in top_pairs[:5]:
            src = int(src)
            dst = int(dst)
            gm, gs, gd = golden_search_move(B, a, tmp, sec_idx, src, dst, n, gold_iterations)
            if np.isfinite(gm):
                if gm < bm - obj_tol or (gm <= bm + near_tol and gs < bs - sec_tol):
                    bm, bs, bsrc, bdst, bd = gm, gs, src, dst, gd

        if bsrc >= 0 and bd > 0.0:
            if bm < cur_obj - obj_tol or (bm <= cur_obj + near_tol and bs < cur_sec - sec_tol):
                add = 2.0 * bd
                B[bdst:bdst + n] += add * a
                B[bsrc:bsrc + n] -= add * a
                d2 = bd * bd
                B[2 * bdst] += d2
                B[2 * bsrc] += d2
                B[bsrc + bdst] -= 2.0 * d2

                a[bsrc] -= bd
                a[bdst] += bd
                if a[bsrc] < 1e-18:
                    a[bsrc] = 0.0
                if a[bdst] < 0.0:
                    a[bdst] = 0.0

                cur_obj = float(B.max())
                accepts += 1
                no_internal = 0
                if cur_obj < best_obj - obj_tol:
                    best_obj = cur_obj
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    if verbose:
                        print("    local obj " + repr(round(2.0 * n * best_obj, 9)) +
                              " t=" + str(round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1)) + "s")
        else:
            no_internal += 1

        if verbose and no_internal > 0 and no_internal % 200 == 0:
            print("    local stall=" + str(no_internal) +
                  " t=" + str(round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1)) + "s")

    Bbest = fast_autocorr(best_a)
    objbest = float(Bbest.max())
    Bcur = fast_autocorr(a)
    objcur = float(Bcur.max())
    if objcur < objbest - obj_tol:
        best_a = a
        objbest = objcur
    score = 2.0 * n * objbest
    return norm_sum1(best_a), float(score)


def mirror_opt(a0, time_limit, beta_start=30.0, beta_end=4000.0,
               top_frac_hi=0.03, top_frac_lo=0.006, target=0.12,
               verbose=True, seed=0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    L = 2 * n - 1
    m = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()

    floor = 1e-12 / n
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        a = default_sequence(n)
    else:
        a = a / s

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)[:L]
    cur_max = float(c.max())
    best_m = cur_max
    best_a = a.copy()
    loga = np.log(a)

    beta = float(beta_start)
    base_factor = 1.0
    reject = 0
    it = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        it += 1
        if beta < 200.0:
            frac = float(top_frac_hi)
        elif beta < 1200.0:
            frac = 0.5 * (float(top_frac_hi) + float(top_frac_lo))
        else:
            frac = float(top_frac_lo)

        top_m = max(30, int(n * frac))
        if top_m >= L:
            idx = np.arange(L)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, L - top_m)[L - top_m:]

        vals = c[idx]
        cmax = float(vals.max())
        shifted = beta * (vals - cmax)
        shifted = np.clip(shifted, -300.0, 300.0)
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[idx] = np.exp(shifted)
        psum = float(p.sum())
        if psum > 0.0 and np.isfinite(psum):
            p /= psum
        else:
            pk = int(np.argmax(c))
            p[pk] = 1.0

        P = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
        corr = np.fft.irfft(P * A.conj(), m)[:n]
        g = 2.0 * corr
        g -= g.mean()
        rg = float(g.max() - g.min())

        if rg <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(rg):
            reject += 1
        else:
            lr = target * base_factor / rg
            if not np.isfinite(lr) or lr <= 0.0:
                lr = 1.0
            lr = min(lr, 1e6)

            z = loga - lr * g
            zmax = float(z.max())
            if not np.isfinite(zmax):
                z = loga.copy()
            else:
                z = z - zmax
            z = np.maximum(z, -50.0)
            a_try = np.exp(z)
            st = float(a_try.sum())

            if st > 0.0 and np.isfinite(st):
                a_try = a_try / st
                loga_try = z - np.log(st)
                A_try = np.fft.rfft(a_try, m)
                c_try = np.fft.irfft(A_try * A_try, m)[:L]
                m_try = float(c_try.max())

                if m_try < cur_max - 1e-15 or m_try < best_m - 1e-15:
                    a = a_try
                    A = A_try
                    c = c_try
                    cur_max = m_try
                    loga = loga_try
                    if m_try < best_m - 1e-15:
                        best_m = m_try
                        best_a = a.copy()
                        reject = 0
                    if m_try < best_m:
                        base_factor = min(1.5, base_factor * 1.08)
                    else:
                        base_factor = 1.0
                    beta = min(float(beta_end), beta * 1.005)
                else:
                    reject += 1
                    base_factor = max(0.2, base_factor * 0.7)
                    if reject > 250:
                        beta = max(10.0, beta * 0.5)
                        base_factor = 1.0
                        improved = False
                        xax = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
                        for _ in range(6):
                            sig = 0.03 * rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0)
                            h = rng.normal(0.0, sig, n)
                            h = h + sig * rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0) * np.cos(np.pi * xax)
                            zp = loga + h
                            zp = zp - float(zp.max())
                            zp = np.maximum(zp, -50.0)
                            ap = np.exp(zp)
                            sp = float(ap.sum())
                            if sp <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sp):
                                continue
                            ap = ap / sp
                            Ap = np.fft.rfft(ap, m)
                            cp = np.fft.irfft(Ap * Ap, m)[:L]
                            mp = float(cp.max())
                            if mp < best_m - 1e-15 or mp < cur_max - 1e-15:
                                a = ap
                                A = Ap
                                c = cp
                                cur_max = mp
                                loga = np.log(ap)
                                improved = True
                                if mp < best_m - 1e-15:
                                    best_m = mp
                                    best_a = a.copy()
                                break
                        if not improved:
                            a = best_a.copy()
                            A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
                            c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)[:L]
                            cur_max = float(c.max())
                            loga = np.log(a)
                            reject = 0

        if it % 500 == 0:
            beta = min(float(beta_end), beta * 1.01)

        if verbose and it % 2000 == 0:
            print("    mirror t=" + str(it) +
                  " (" + str(round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1)) + "s): score=" +
                  repr(round(2.0 * n * cur_max, 6)) +
                  " beta=" + str(round(beta, 1)))

    a = norm_sum1(a)
    best_a = norm_sum1(best_a)
    sc1 = score_normalized(a)
    sc2 = score_normalized(best_a)
    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 <= sc1:
        return best_a, sc2
    return a, sc1


def optimize_candidate(a0, time_alloc, seed=0, verbose=False):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    sc = score_normalized(a)

    t_local1 = max(0.0, min(float(time_alloc) * 0.20, 120.0))
    if t_local1 > 5.0:
        a, sc = local_search(a, t_local1, verbose=verbose, seed=int(seed))

    rem = float(time_alloc) - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    t_mir = max(0.0, min(rem * 0.60, 1200.0))
    if t_mir > 15.0:
        a, sc = mirror_opt(a, t_mir, verbose=verbose, seed=int(seed) + 1)

    rem = float(time_alloc) - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    t_local2 = max(0.0, rem * 0.90)
    if t_local2 > 5.0:
        a, sc = local_search(a, t_local2, verbose=verbose, seed=int(seed) + 2)

    rem = float(time_alloc) - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 5.0:
        a, sc = local_search(a, rem * 0.80, verbose=False, seed=int(seed) + 3)

    a = norm_sum1(a)
    sc = score_normalized(a)
    return a, float(sc)


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1800.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 1800.0
    hard_deadline = t_global + max(20.0, budget - 60.0)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Parent / previous state of art, if available.
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size > 0 and float(p.sum()) > 1e-10:
            parent = norm_sum1(p)
            ps = score_normalized(parent)
            best_seq = parent
            best_score = ps
            print("Parent: n=" + str(parent.size) + ", score=" + repr(ps))
    except Exception:
        best_seq = None

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = default_sequence(6700)
        best_score = score_normalized(best_seq)
        print("Default start: score=" + repr(best_score))

    if best_seq.size > 12000:
        best_seq = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, 12000)
        best_score = score_normalized(best_seq)

    pool = [(best_seq, best_score, "best0")]
    n_base = best_seq.size

    # Often slightly longer lengths are useful if they remain moderate.
    if n_base < 10000:
        n_long = int(min(10000, max(n_base + 1500, int(n_base * 1.5))))
    elif n_base < 12000:
        n_long = 12000
    else:
        n_long = n_base
    if n_long != n_base:
        add_candidate(pool, interpolate_sequence(best_seq, n_long), "long")

    # Generate parent perturbations and parametric candidates.
    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 80 and best_seq.size > 0:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(2024)
        n_param = n_base
        if n_param > 10000:
            n_param = 10000
        if n_param < 3500:
            n_param = 3500

        basis_par = get_basis(n_param, "mixed")
        R = basis_par.shape[0]

        pdeadline = time.monotonic() + max(7.0, min(35.0, remaining * 0.12))
        n_perturb = 18 if n_param > 9000 else 36
        for _ in range(n_perturb):
            if time.monotonic() > pdeadline:
                break
            theta = rng.uniform(-1.7, 1.7, R)
            seq = parent_perturb_seq(best_seq, theta, basis_par, 2.0)
            add_candidate(pool, seq, "parentperturb")

        rdeadline = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, min(25.0, remaining * 0.10))
        for mode in ("anti", "mixed"):
            basis = get_basis(n_param, mode)
            Rmode = basis.shape[0]
            n_rand = 10 if n_param > 9000 else 20
            for _ in range(n_rand):
                if time.monotonic() > rdeadline:
                    break
                theta = rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0, Rmode)
                seq = param_to_seq(theta, n_param, basis, 3.5)
                add_candidate(pool, seq, mode + "rand")

    # Optional deterministic-ish global optimizer on the parametric family.
    if sopt is not None and n_base <= 10000:
        remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining > 240:
            basis_de = get_basis(n_base, "mixed")
            Rde = basis_de.shape[0]
            bounds = [(-2.0, 2.0)] * Rde
            try:
                res = sopt.differential_evolution(
                    de_objective,
                    bounds,
                    args=(n_base, basis_de, 3.5),
                    maxiter=45,
                    popsize=10,
                    seed=7,
                    tol=1e-5,
                    polish=False
                )
                seq_de = param_to_seq(res.x, n_base, basis_de, 3.5)
                add_candidate(pool, seq_de, "de")
            except Exception:
                pass

    pool.sort(key=candidate_key)

    # Keep a small diverse top list, ensuring the long candidate has a slot
    # if it is not far behind.
    top = pool[:8]
    long_cand = None
    has_long = False
    for item in top:
        if str(item[2]).startswith("long"):
            has_long = True
    for item in pool:
        if str(item[2]).startswith("long"):
            long_cand = item
            break
    if long_cand is not None and not has_long and long_cand[1] <= best_score + 0.02:
        if len(top) < 8:
            top.append(long_cand)
        else:
            top[-1] = long_cand
    pool = top

    # Quick local polish of the most promising candidates.
    quick_deadline = hard_deadline - max(180.0, (hard_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.45)
    for i in range(min(4, len(pool))):
        if time.monotonic() > quick_deadline:
            break
        a, sc, label = pool[i]
        t_fast = min(40.0, max(0.0, (hard_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.20))
        if t_fast <= 3.0:
            break
        a_out, sc_out = local_search(a, t_fast, verbose=False, seed=20 + i)
        pool[i] = (a_out, sc_out, label)
        if sc_out < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_seq = a_out
            best_score = sc_out
            print("  NEW BEST [" + label + "]: " + repr(sc_out) +
                  " (n=" + str(a_out.size) + ", t=" + str(round(time.monotonic() - t_global, 1)) + "s)")
        pool.sort(key=candidate_key)

    # Main deep optimization.
    weights = (0.45, 0.25, 0.15, 0.10, 0.05, 0.05)
    for i in range(min(5, len(pool))):
        remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 80:
            break
        w = float(weights[i]) if i < len(weights) else 0.05
        alloc = remaining * w
        if i == 0:
            alloc = min(alloc, 900.0)
        else:
            alloc = min(alloc, 420.0)
        if alloc < 20:
            continue
        a, sc, label = pool[i]
        print("Deep [" + label + "] alloc=" + str(round(alloc, 1)) + "s, current=" + repr(sc))
        a_out, sc_out = optimize_candidate(a, alloc, seed=100 + i)
        if sc_out < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_seq = a_out
            best_score = sc_out
            print("  NEW BEST [" + label + "]: " + repr(sc_out) +
                  " (n=" + str(a_out.size) + ", t=" + str(round(time.monotonic() - t_global, 1)) + "s)")

    # If time remains, try a longer interpolated extension of the architectically best sequence.
    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 250 and best_seq.size < 12000:
        new_n = int(min(12000, best_seq.size * 1.25 + 1))
        if new_n <= best_seq.size + 100:
            new_n = int(min(12000, best_seq.size + 1500))
        if new_n > best_seq.size:
            a_long = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, new_n)
            t_alloc = min(remaining - 60.0, 400.0)
            if t_alloc > 30:
                a_out, sc_out = optimize_candidate(a_long, t_alloc, seed=999)
                if sc_out < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_seq = a_out
                    best_score = sc_out
                    print("  NEW BEST [length-extension]: " + repr(sc_out) +
                          " (n=" + str(a_out.size) + ", t=" + str(round(time.monotonic() - t_global, 1)) + "s)")

    # Final exact polish on the current global best.
    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 30:
        t_fin = min(remaining - 15.0, 220.0)
        if t_fin > 5:
            a_out, sc_out = local_search(best_seq, t_fin, verbose=False, seed=555)
            if sc_out < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_seq = a_out
                best_score = sc_out
                print("  NEW BEST [final local]: " + repr(sc_out))

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = default_sequence(6700)

    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    best_seq = np.nan_to_num(best_seq, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    best_seq = np.clip(best_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if best_seq.size > 12000:
        best_seq = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, 12000)
    best_seq = norm_sum1(best_seq)
    final_score = score_normalized(best_seq)
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
    print("FINAL: score=" + repr(final_score) + ", n=" + str(best_seq.size) +
          ", time=" + str(round(elapsed, 1)) + "s")

    # The evaluator accepts Python floats; keep them valid and nonnegative.
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
